Kinh tế & Shop — evidence và hành động batch06

14 nhóm · 206 UC nguồn · 154 ca cần bằng chứng đầu batch06. Hiện tại: 58 quan sát một phần / 148 cần bằng chứng. Batch06 đã áp dụng 9 record runtime và 30 native addenda.

Đủ 154 dòng nghĩa là mỗi ca có điều kiện, hành động và yêu cầu bằng chứng; không nghĩa là đã test toàn bộ. Runtime, native cô lập và kế hoạch nguồn được tách rõ. Không chi tiền IAP.

Ads-CASE-002 — native bổ sung

New actual AreAdsEnabledForUser0x3F9D864 requires NOT alternative Android store, enabled AB group and NOT backend-disabled. All eight supplied bool combinations assert short-circuit result. Current areAdsPrepared=false captured separately.

  • Store/cohort/backend getters are explicit stubs. This does not execute SDK initialization/preparation, server disable update, consent or ad loading. Current unprepared state is not an SDK failure.
  • Bounded new06 subset only; no whole-UC verification or live purchase/SDK/server-population claim.

Ads-CASE-007 — native bổ sung

New outer Ads enable gate execution is combined with fresh typed baseline AB dictionary: neither205 nor206 exists, so native selector default0. Historical actual provider initializer keys2..6 are reused only as known native source, not new06 coverage.

  • Live provider dictionary contents were not recaptured. No positive cohort, SDK/reward branch or backend population coverage. Missing assignments do not mean server globally disabled Ads.
  • Bounded new06 subset only; no whole-UC verification or live purchase/SDK/server-population claim.

Ads-CASE-013 — native bổ sung

New actual OnRewardedAdClosed0x3FA1E88 snapshots didReceiveReward before ClearRewardCache, passes original bool to optional callback, then requests user sync. Four received/callback combinations assert order and preserved bool.

  • ClearRewardCache and user sync are supplied stubs; static helper review confirms flag clear. No actual completed ad, callback authenticity, reward application, duplicate-callback protection or backend acknowledgement.
  • Bounded new06 subset only; no whole-UC verification or live purchase/SDK/server-population claim.

Offers-CASE-006 — runtime một phần

OOM10006 tự hiện Royal Favor:60%OFF,1000coin+Hammer1+5moves cho level này, nút giá29.000VND; timer59→13→Finished. Khi Finished, nút giá chuyển xám nhưng vẫn nhận tap và hiện toast “This offer has expired!”. Offer vẫn hiển thị. Typed EgoInProgress/ShowEgoForCurrentProgress còn true, PurchasedThisSession=false và endTime không đổi trước/sau expiry. Không mua.

Một lần xuất hiện tự nhiên, nội dung/giá hiển thị trong game, countdown qua các frame, trạng thái Finished và xử lý tap sau expiry. Phân biệt regular SpecialOffer confignull với RoyalFavorEgo đang hiện.

  • One naturally shown offer; install-age, retry/RNG50percent gate and total allowed frequency not isolated.
  • Game-button price only; no fresh GooglePlay quote/receipt/payment/grant.
  • Timer samples bound observed countdown/expiry, not continuous exact configured duration.
  • Grey is not noninteractive: click handler rejects with toast. No disappearance/reset claim; Ego flags remain true after expiry.
  • Regular SpecialOffer confignull/eventId0 coexists with this RoyalFavorEgo branch; do not infer it globallydisabled.

WelcomeBackGift-CASE-001 — native bổ sung

UpdateLastActiveDay0x304942C writes current day if TimeHelper.IsTimeValidatedByBackend byte10 istrue, or ShouldUpdateLastActiveDateFromClient0x304A63C istrue. Clientfallback requires LastActiveDate!=default and ToDays(CurrentDate)>ToDays(LastActiveDate). Missinglastdate doesnot initialize through thisfallback. CurrentDate0x3048C68 calls TimeHelper.CurrentServerTimeInMs thenTimeUtil.ToDateTime. UpdateDayDifference0x30494D8 writes-1 formissingLastActiveDate, otherwise difference.TotalDays to manager+30.

  • Current39933 subset only; not wholeUC pass/allcohorts or backend reachability.
  • Nativecontrol-flow review only; no live dateupdate/callback trace, natural15/30/90day return, fullclockvalidation or packedday codec test. CurrentServerTime helper internals and lifecyclecaller ordering remain separate.
